### Vampire buildings

haven_defenses_01 = {
	internal_slots = 1
	construction_time = @camp_building_main_slot_construction_duration_t1
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }

	cost = { gold = camp_fire_01_domicile_building_gold_cost_value }
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = camp_fire_01_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}
	
	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	
	character_modifier = {
		enemy_hostile_scheme_phase_duration_add = 5
		stress_loss_mult = 0.05
		personal_scheme_phase_duration_add = -2
	}

	parameters = {
		#vampblood_building = yes
	}
	
	ai_value = { value = camp_fire_main_path_value }

# Night Landed

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			owner = { POD_day_background_trigger = no }
			POD_domicile_uses_landed_yurt_assets = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_knight_yurt.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/character_focused_warfare_yurt_n.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/character_focused_warfare_yurt_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_campfire"
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			owner = { POD_day_background_trigger = no }
			POD_domicile_uses_landed_mena_assets = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_watchtower.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_watchtower_mena_n.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_watchtower_mena_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_campfire"
	}
	
	asset = {

			owner = { POD_day_background_trigger = no }
			POD_domicile_uses_landed_western_assets = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_watchtower.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_watchtower_western_n.dds"

haven_defenses_02 = {
	construction_time = @camp_building_main_slot_construction_duration_t2
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	previous_building = haven_defenses_01
	internal_slots = 1

	cost = { gold = camp_fire_02_domicile_building_gold_cost_value }
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = camp_fire_02_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			add = camp_fire_01_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes

	}

	parameters = {
		#vampblood_building = yes
	}
	
	ai_value = { value = camp_fire_main_path_value }

haven_defenses_live_entertainment = {
	slot_type = internal
	construction_time = @camp_building_internal_slot_construction_duration_t1
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	previous_building = haven_defenses_01

	cost = { gold = camp_fire_trailing_musicians_domicile_building_gold_cost_value }
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = camp_fire_trailing_musicians_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	
	character_modifier = {
		stress_gain_mult = -0.05
	}

	parameters = {
		camp_unlocks_master_bard_officer = yes
		camp_improved_musician_scheme_agent = yes
		#vampblood_building = yes
	}
	
	ai_value = {
		value = camp_building_ai_base_t4
		if = {
			limit = {
				owner = { has_lifestyle = diplomacy_lifestyle }
			}
			add = camp_building_ai_lifestyle_bias_value
		}
		multiply = 10
	}
	
	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_trailing_musicians.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_roaring_campfire.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_roaring_campfire_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_campfire"
	}
}

		gold = {
			value = barber_tent_surgeons_tools_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}
	can_construct = {
		has_trait = vampire
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		has_trait = vampire
	}
	character_modifier = {
		hard_casualty_modifier = -0.2
	}

	parameters = {
		camp_improved_court_physician_aptitude = yes
		#vampblood_building = yes
	}
	
	ai_value = {
		value = camp_building_ai_base_t5
		if = {
			limit = {
				owner = { has_lifestyle = learning_lifestyle }
			}
			add = camp_building_ai_lifestyle_bias_value
		}
		multiply = 10
	}
	
	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_surgeons_tools.dds"

	}
}

		gold = {
			value = baggage_train_03_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			add = baggage_train_02_domicile_building_gold_cost_value

	internal_slots = 1

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
		# safeguard so this can be rebuilt after going landed, even after spending the trait xp
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					is_target_in_variable_list = {
						name = saved_haven_buildings
						target = flag:vampire_military_order_021
					}
				}
			}
			has_trait_xp = {
				trait = ashen_adventurer
				track = ashen_grandmaster
				value >= 15
			}
		}

vampire_military_ghoul_quarters = {
	slot_type = internal
	construction_time = @camp_building_internal_slot_construction_duration_t2
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	previous_building = vampire_military_order_01

	cost = { gold = proving_grounds_horse_run_domicile_building_gold_cost_value }
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = proving_grounds_horse_run_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	character_modifier = {
		POD_ghoul_maa_max_size_add = 1
		POD_ghoul_maa_maintenance_mult = -0.1
		POD_ghoul_maa_recruitment_cost_mult = -0.1
	}
	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	parameters = {
		#vampblood_building = yes
	}
	
	ai_value = {
		value = camp_building_ai_base_t1
		if = {
			limit = {
				owner = { has_lifestyle = martial_lifestyle }
			}
			add = camp_building_ai_lifestyle_bias_value
		}
		multiply = 10
		if = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					number_maa_regiments_of_base_type:light_cavalry <= 0
				}
			}
			multiply = 0
		}
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_guardhouse.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_proving_grounds.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_proving_grounds_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_proving_ground"
	}
}

	construction_time = @camp_building_main_slot_construction_duration_t1
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }

	cost = { gold = proving_grounds_01_domicile_building_gold_cost_value }
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = proving_grounds_01_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}
	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	
	character_modifier = {
		domicile_monthly_prestige_add = 0.25
		personal_scheme_phase_duration_add = -5
	}

	parameters = {
		#vampblood_building = yes
		camp_better_scout_for_talent = yes
		fireside_chat_intent_unlock = yes
	}

	ai_value = { value = mess_tent_main_path_value }

# Night Landed

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			owner = { POD_day_background_trigger = no }
			POD_domicile_uses_landed_yurt_assets = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_diplomacy_yurt.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/external_diplomacy_yurt_n.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/external_diplomacy_yurt_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_campfire"
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			owner = { POD_day_background_trigger = no }
			POD_domicile_uses_landed_mena_assets = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_nightly_debates.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_garden_mena_n.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_garden_mena_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_campfire"
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			owner = { POD_day_background_trigger = no }
			POD_domicile_uses_landed_western_assets = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_nightly_debates.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_garden_western_n.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_garden_western_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_campfire"
	}
	
	asset = {

			POD_domicile_uses_landed_byzantine_assets = yes
		}

		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_garden_byzantine_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_campfire"

vampire_ambassador_02 = {
	construction_time = @camp_building_main_slot_construction_duration_t2
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	previous_building = vampire_ambassador_01
	internal_slots = 1

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	
	cost = { gold = proving_grounds_02_domicile_building_gold_cost_value }
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = proving_grounds_02_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			add = proving_grounds_01_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	character_modifier = {
		domicile_monthly_prestige_add = 0.25
		owned_personal_scheme_success_chance_max_add = 5
	}

	parameters = {
		#vampblood_building = yes
		camp_better_scout_for_talent = yes
		fireside_chat_intent_unlock = yes
	}
	
	ai_value = { value = mess_tent_main_path_value }

		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
		# safeguard so this can be rebuilt after going landed, even after spending the trait xp
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					is_target_in_variable_list = {
						name = saved_haven_buildings
						target = flag:vampire_ambassador_021
					}
				}
			}
			has_trait_xp = {
				trait = ashen_adventurer
				track = ashen_ambassador
				value >= 15
			}
		}
		#is_landless_adventurer = yes
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes

			value = proving_grounds_03_domicile_building_gold_cost_value

			add = proving_grounds_01_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

vampire_cult_021 = {
	construction_time = @camp_building_main_slot_construction_duration_t2
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	previous_building = vampire_cult_01
	internal_slots = 1

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
		has_trait_xp = {
			trait = ashen_adventurer
			track = ashen_cult
			value >= 15
		}
		#is_landless_adventurer = yes
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	
	cost = { gold = proving_grounds_02_domicile_building_gold_cost_value }
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = proving_grounds_02_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			add = proving_grounds_01_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	character_modifier = {
		domicile_monthly_piety_add = 0.5
		owned_hostile_scheme_success_chance_max_add = 10
	}

	parameters = {
		ashen_adventurer_hint = yes
		#vampblood_building = yes
		camp_unlocks_poisoner_scheme_agents = yes
		camp_unlocks_camp_priest_officer = yes
		camp_enable_conversion = yes
	}
	
	ai_value = { value = mess_tent_main_path_value }
	
# Night Landed

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			owner = { POD_day_background_trigger = no }
			POD_domicile_uses_landed_yurt_assets = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_righteous_yurt.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/mystical_magical_yurt_n.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/mystical_magical_yurt_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_campfire"
	}

vampire_cult_041 = {
	construction_time = @camp_building_main_slot_construction_duration_t4
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	previous_building = vampire_cult_031
	internal_slots = 2

	cost = { gold = proving_grounds_04_domicile_building_gold_cost_value }
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = proving_grounds_04_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			add = proving_grounds_03_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			add = proving_grounds_02_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			add = proving_grounds_01_domicile_building_gold_cost_value
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}
	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
		has_trait_xp = {
			trait = ashen_adventurer
			track = ashen_cult
			value >= 45
		}
		#is_landless_adventurer = yes
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	character_modifier = {
		domicile_monthly_piety_add = 0.5
		owned_hostile_scheme_success_chance_add = 10
	}

	parameters = {
		ashen_adventurer_hint = yes
		#vampblood_building = yes
		camp_unlocks_poisoner_scheme_agents = yes
		camp_unlocks_camp_priest_officer = yes
		camp_enable_conversion = yes
	}
	
	ai_value = { value = mess_tent_main_path_value }

vampire_tzimisce_fleshcrafting_laboratory_01 = {
	slot_type = internal
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	construction_time = @vampire_clan_internal_construction_duration_t1
	previous_building = camp_main_01

	cost = {
		gold = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t1
		piety = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t1
	}
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
		piety = {
			value = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = saved_haven_buildings target = flag:vampire_tzimisce_fleshcrafting_laboratory_01 } } }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = POD_requirements_tzimisce_bloodline
				is_tzimisce_clan_or_bloodline_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}

	parameters = {
		domicile_stronger_ghouls_t1 = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
		POD_ghoul_maa_recruitment_cost_mult = -0.1
	}

	on_complete = {
		scope:owner = {
			every_relation = {
				type = ghoul_thrall
				add_character_modifier = POD_domicile_ghoul_modifier_t1
			}
		}
	}

	ai_value = { value = vampire_clan_internal_ai_value }

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_morticians_tools.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_barber_tent"
	}
}

vampire_tzimisce_fleshcrafting_laboratory_03 = {
	slot_type = internal
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	construction_time = @vampire_clan_internal_construction_duration_t3
	previous_building = vampire_tzimisce_fleshcrafting_laboratory_02

	cost = {
		gold = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t3
		piety = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t3
	}
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t1
			add = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t2
			add = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t3
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
		piety = {
			value = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t1
			add = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t2
			add = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t3
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
		domicile = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = camp_main_03 }
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}

	parameters = {
		domicile_stronger_ghouls_t2 = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
		POD_ghoul_maa_recruitment_cost_mult = -0.1
	}

	on_complete = {
		scope:owner = {
			every_relation = {
				type = ghoul_thrall
				remove_character_modifier = POD_domicile_ghoul_modifier_t1
				add_character_modifier = POD_domicile_ghoul_modifier_t2
			}
		}
	}

	ai_value = { value = vampire_clan_internal_ai_value }

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_morticians_tools.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_barber_tent"
	}
}

vampire_brujah_agora_01 = {
	slot_type = internal
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	construction_time = @vampire_clan_internal_construction_duration_t1
	previous_building = camp_main_01

	cost = {
		gold = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t1
		piety = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t1
	}
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
		piety = {
			value = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = saved_haven_buildings target = flag:vampire_brujah_agora_01 } } }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = POD_requirements_brujah_clan
				is_brujah_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}

	parameters = {
		domicile_brujah_panegyra_discount_t1 = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
		monthly_learning_lifestyle_xp_gain_mult = 0.10
		knight_effectiveness_mult = 0.05
	}

	ai_value = { value = vampire_clan_internal_ai_value }

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_martial_study.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_barber_tent"
	}
}

vampire_giovanni_counting_house_01 = {
	slot_type = internal
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	construction_time = @vampire_clan_internal_construction_duration_t1
	previous_building = camp_main_01

	cost = {
		gold = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t1
		piety = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t1
	}
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
		piety = {
			value = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = saved_haven_buildings target = flag:vampire_giovanni_counting_house_01 } } }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = POD_requirements_giovanni_clan
				is_giovanni_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	parameters = {
		domicile_giovanni_family_embrace_stat_t1 = yes
		domicile_giovanni_family_embrace_cost_t1 = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
		domain_tax_mult = 0.02
	}

	ai_value = { value = vampire_clan_internal_ai_value }

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_market.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_barber_tent"
	}
}

vampire_nagaraja_pilgrimage_alcove_01 = {
	slot_type = internal
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	construction_time = @vampire_clan_internal_construction_duration_t1
	previous_building = camp_main_01

	cost = {
		gold = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t1
		piety = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t1
	}
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = @vampire_clan_internal_gold_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
		piety = {
			value = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = saved_haven_buildings target = flag:vampire_nagaraja_pilgrimage_alcove_01 } } }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = POD_requirements_nagaraja_clan
				is_nagaraja_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}

	parameters = {
		domicile_stronger_wraiths_t1 = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
		POD_wraith_maa_damage_mult = 0.05
		POD_terrain_necromancy_wraith_advantage = 1
	}

	on_complete = {
		scope:owner = {
			every_relation = {
				type = bound_wraith
				add_character_modifier = POD_domicile_wraith_modifier_t1
			}
		}
	}

	ai_value = { value = vampire_clan_internal_ai_value }

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_lifestyle_xp_yurt.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_barber_tent"
	}

		}

	}

	}

		POD_wraith_maa_damage_mult = 0.05
	}

	on_complete = {

			every_relation = {
				type = bound_wraith
				remove_character_modifier = POD_domicile_wraith_modifier_t2

	}

			floor = yes
		}
		piety = {
			value = @vampire_clan_internal_misc_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = saved_haven_buildings target = flag:vampire_baali_pit_01 } } }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = POD_requirements_baali_clan
				is_baali_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_vamp_blood = yes
	}
	parameters = {
		domicile_baali_infernal_bonus_t1 = yes
		domicile_baali_sabotage_reduction = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
		general_opinion = -5
		dynasty_opinion = 5
	}

	ai_value = { value = vampire_clan_internal_ai_value }

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_raid_main_yurt.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_barber_mask.png"
